Components/codes: `room-1:COLD_ROOM`, `probe-1:TEMPERATURE_PROBE`, `ambient-1:TEMPERATURE_PROBE`, `compressor-1:COMPRESSOR`, `transfer-1:TRANSFER_SWITCH`, `generator-1:GENERATOR`, `bus-1:AC_BUS`, `grid-1:METER`. The door sensor is a declared device on `room-1`; `door-1` is the scenario's forcing address, resolved to that sensor and room, not a second Foundation component. Use the COLD-001 directed topology with these codes. Declare precisely the eleven live channels from `V4_CHANNEL_MAPPINGS` excluding `operator-log/temperature-event`; map their component to the corresponding code. The periodic source is the one registered simulated source/gateway produced by `import_site` and `binding_conversion.convert`, never a test-injected `BindingBasis`.

Thirteen Foundation properties, all frozen and required: on `room-1`, `cold-thermal-capacity=1.2 kWh/degC`, `cold-heat-leak=0.08 kW/degC`, `cold-door-heat=0.02 kWh/min`, `cold-control-low=2 degC`, `cold-control-high=4 degC`, `cold-policy-low=2 degC`, `cold-policy-high=4 degC`, `cold-min-excursion=15 min`, `cold-return-hysteresis=0.2 degC`; on `probe-1`, `cold-probe-error=0.1 degC`; on `compressor-1`, `cold-cooling-capacity=3 kW`, `cold-compressor-start-delay=1 min`; on `generator-1`, `cold-start-timeout=5 min`. Generator start delay `2 min` and transfer delay `1 min` are versioned `cold-thermal/1` pack constants; they are not invented Foundation properties. The pack's named defaults are used only when constructing a new scenario fixture, not to override Foundation. Existing COLD-001/002 bytes and W2-01 assertions stay intact.

Set scenario/run start after that instant. Do not mutate an existing revision or change existing W2-01 snapshot hashes. ## Scenario and observation documents

Create `backend/assetops_backend/scenarios/cold_parsing.py:parse_cold_scenario_document` and immutable `ColdScenarioDefinition` as a separate strict parser/model; `scenarios/parsing.py:SCENARIO_KEYS,TIMELINE_ENTRY_KEYS,STATE_EFFECT_KEYS` and legacy `ScenarioDefinition` do not accept cold keys. Files `config/scenarios/cold-{normal,excursion,backup-failed,missing-probe,late-reading,contradictory}.yaml` have exactly: `schema: cold-scenario/1`, `scenario_id: cold-<case>`, `version: 1`, `site_code: COLD-LAB-001`, `model_profile: cold-thermal/1`, `publication_profile: cold-publication/1`, `observation_profile: cold-observation/1`, `seed: 20417`, `start: 2026-10-06T19:00:00Z`, `end: 2026-10-06T20:00:00Z`, `initial_temperature_deg_c` (canonical decimal string), `ambient_deg_c` (canonical decimal string), `backup_enabled: true`, `forcings: []`, `observations: []`. Forcing row exact keys `{at,kind,asset,value}`, `kind=GRID_LOSS|GRID_RESTORE|GENERATOR_FAILURE|DOOR_OPEN|DOOR_CLOSE`, with `value=LOST|AVAILABLE|FAILED|OPEN|CLOSED` respectively. Observation row exact keys `{at,kind,device_id,action,outcome,release_at}`; `kind=PROBE_REPORT|START_RESULT`, `action=DROP|HOLD|ADD`, `outcome` null except ADD `SUCCEEDED`, `release_at` UTC only for HOLD. Reject extra/duplicate/unsorted/out-of-window rows, unknown assets, noncanonical decimals, or wrong value/action pairs. Parse with `yaml.safe_load`, canonicalize the parsed closed object with `canonical_json`, SHA-256 UTF-8; document comments/formatting do not change scenario digest. In `scenarios/adapters/yaml_shipped_scenario_store.py`, dispatch files by the exact `schema` key; return the typed union through `scenarios/ports.py:ScenarioDefinitionRepository` and `scenarios/service.py:ScenarioCatalogService`. `runs/service.py` dispatches cold definitions to a cold-only freeze path. Add the six to the Lab choices only for the registered fixture; never route them through the old timeline parser.

Base `normal`: initial 3.00, ambient 10, loss 19:05, restore 19:35. All others: initial 3.80, ambient 20, loss and generator failure 19:00. `backup-failed` adds door open 19:10 and close 19:11; `missing-probe` has six DROP rows at 19:15,20,25,30,35,40; `late-reading` has HOLD at 19:20 released 20:10; `contradictory` has ADD successful START_RESULT at 19:05. Default observations empty. `cold-observation/1` in `simulator/assetops_simulator/observation/transform.py` applies zero bias, half-even 0.1Â°C quantization and +3-second publication latency. DROP emits nothing. HOLD keeps original `source_time` and is released at 20:10. ADD creates a second GOOD witnessed controller event with a distinct event ID and the same attempt correlation; it does not alter physical state. Probe failure emits one `UNAVAILABLE`/`SENSOR_FAILURE` point, then no numeric readings (unit test forcing only). No manual log is emitted or bound.

Extend `FrozenScenarioBinding` in `backend/assetops_backend/runs/models.py` with optional `scenario_sha256`; extend `FrozenProfileBinding` with optional `observation_profile_id`, `observation_profile_version`, `observation_sha256`. Freeze exact `cold-scenario/1`, `cold-observation/1`, `cold-thermal/1`, `cold-publication/1`, seed, effective Foundation revision and v4 binding revision before Draft in `runs/service.py`; `runs/parsing.py:render_run,parse_run` writes these four added keys only for cold (omit, never null, for older runs). Define `observation_sha256` as SHA-256 of the UTF-8 bytes of `canonical_json({"observation_profile":"cold-observation/1","observations":<the parsed document's observations array>})`. Preserve row order and the parser's closed, normalized row values; the empty array still has a digest. Do not include forcings, private state, YAML formatting or the rest of the scenario document in this observation digest; `scenario_sha256` separately covers the entire parsed cold document. `runs/source_publication.py:run_identity_sha256` then naturally covers both digests. Add cold-only validation that all four are present and SHA strings lowercase 64 hex. Keep old serialized run and fingerprint golden vectors byte-identical.

The frozen run identity carries Foundation revision ID and content SHA, binding revision ID, scenario ID and SHA, profile IDs/versions and observation SHA, seed and requested timing; it does not embed the thirteen Foundation property values or cold document contents. At execution the host re-reads the exact frozen Foundation revision and cold scenario document, verifies the Foundation revision ID and content SHA and the document's `scenario_sha256`, and recomputes `observation_sha256` from that document. It extracts the thirteen values from that verified Foundation revision and uses the verified document for forcings and observation rows. Missing, changed or mismatched inputs refuse execution before any public Stage output; no current-revision or fixture-default substitution is permitted.

**Cold pack transition closure.** `GENERATOR_FAILURE` is a latent private start fault: the outage still produces `START_REQUEST`, the generator enters `STARTING`, and at the effective Foundation start timeout it becomes `FAILED` and emits `START_RESULT/FAILED` (five minutes, 19:05, in the failure fixtures). On grid restore, the transfer returns to `GRID` immediately and emits `TRANSFER_RESULT/SUCCEEDED` when the configured transfer position sensor is present; the generator stops. While the compressor is `RUNNING` on a powered bus, its published power in kW equals the effective `cold-cooling-capacity` value; otherwise published power is zero. Public grid power and transfer events for one outage use its outage correlation ID; door events use their separate door-cycle correlation. The registered ambient probe publishes an ambient temperature point every five minutes, including the closing boundary, through its bound channel. These are public observations of the resulting transitions, not reports of the private forcing.

**Lab choice and run-request closure (2026-10-17).** Keep the existing `GET /api/simulator-lab/scenarios` response and `GET /api/simulator-lab/scenarios/{scenario_id}` detail legacy-only; neither exposes the six cold definitions without a Site selection. Add gated `GET /api/simulator-lab/cold-scenarios?site_id=COLD-LAB-001` in `simulator_lab_api.py`, backed by `ScenarioCatalogService` and the injected `RegisteredBasisPort`. Its response is `{ "site_id": "COLD-LAB-001", "scenarios": [choice, ...] }`, where each `choice` has exactly `scenario_id` (string), `scenario_version` (integer), `display_name` (string), `schema` (`cold-scenario/1`), `model_profile`, `publication_profile`, `observation_profile` (each a string such as `cold-thermal/1`), `start`, `end` (UTC strings), and `seed` (integer). Derive all fields except `display_name` from the saved cold document; labels for the six IDs are `Normal`, `Excursion`, `Backup failed`, `Missing probe`, `Late reading`, and `Contradictory`. Order by `scenario_id`. Return the six only when the requested Site ID is exactly `COLD-LAB-001` and that Site is registered. For any other or unregistered Site, return an empty `scenarios` array; an unreadable scenario/registration store remains an unavailable error, never an empty claim. The Lab choice client calls this read after Site selection and offers the returned cold scenarios only for that selected Site; the unfiltered legacy catalog remains legacy-only. The cold document's `site_code` is a fixture compatibility constraint enforced at run setup, not a general scenario-to-Site binding rule. `RunSetupRequest` keeps its required `model_profile` and `publication_profile` `{profile_id,profile_version}` selections. For a cold run, freeze only when both selections exactly equal the document's `cold-thermal/1` and `cold-publication/1`; the document alone chooses `cold-observation/1`, whose identity and digest are frozen. Also require the request's Site, scenario version, seed, interval and one-minute timestep to match the cold document; refuse any mismatch before Draft. Do not add a separate observation-profile request key or silently substitute profile choices.

## DeviceReport v2 and gateway path

Keep `gateway_report.py:DeviceReport` v1 parser, serializer and `report_from_observation` unchanged. Add `DeviceReportV2`, `device_report_v2_text`, `parse_device_report_v2_text` with schema `{id:'assetops.simulated-device-report',version:2}` and exact keys `schema,report_key,device_id,signal_id,source_time,family,measurement,interval_start,interval_end,value,unit,quality,unavailable_reason,record_kind,event_id,correlation_id,component_id,actor,outcome,session_id,connector_id`. Every key is present; inapplicable fields are null. Cold measurement: `family=MEASUREMENT`, `measurement=STATE_AT_INSTANT|INTERVAL_MEAN`, `unit=degC|state|kW`; events: `family=EVENT`, measurement/interval/value/unit/reason null, kind/outcome from v4 allowlist. Unavailable measurement has null value and `quality=UNAVAILABLE,unavailable_reason=SENSOR_FAILURE`. `report_key` = SHA-256 of canonical `(scenario_sha256,device_id,signal_id,source_time,record_kind,event_id,interval_start)` prefixed `cold:`; deterministic for retransmission. Event ID = `cold:` plus SHA-256 of canonical `(scenario_sha256,device_id,signal_id,transition_ordinal)`; door pair shares a door-cycle correlation, start request/result shares outage/attempt correlation. No run ID/private cause in report.

`gateway/assetops_gateway/service.py:GatewayService._admit_in` dispatches by report schema: v1 -> existing V3 branch; v2 -> V4 only if activated `VerifiedSnapshot.binding_for` carries exact five v4 metadata keys, matched against `V4_CHANNEL_MAPPINGS`. The gateway, not Stage, retains exclusive `(gateway,source,epoch,sequence)` allocation and `v3_message_id` construction (same transport identity algorithm); it constructs `SourceEnvelopeV4`, canonicalizes with `canonical_envelope_v4_text`, and writes existing SQLite `envelope_outbox`. `_check_report` validates event kind/outcome/units and interval bounds using the v4 parser before sequence allocation. `report_admission` deduplicates equal report key+SHA before allocation. `GatewayService.seal,release_run,record_receipt` and `GatewayReader` handle mixed schema batches via Batch V2 header path; no direct host-built canonical envelope or new gateway table. In `host/lab_execution.py:_stage_registered_virtual`, adapt cold public observations to v2 reports and call `GatewayService.admit` with the pinned snapshot and virtual publication clock. Stage is the gateway-sealed public envelope view, not an alternate envelope writer.

.agent\architect-W2-02-q2-log.txt:1762:+**Lab choice and run-request closure (2026-10-17).** Keep the existing `GET /api/simulator-lab/scenarios` response and `GET /api/simulator-lab/scenarios/{scenario_id}` detail legacy-only; neither exposes the six cold definitions without a Site selection. Add gated `GET /api/simulator-lab/cold-scenarios?site_id=COLD-LAB-001` in `simulator_lab_api.py`, backed by `ScenarioCatalogService` and the injected `RegisteredBasisPort`. Its exact response is `{ "site_id": "COLD-LAB-001", "scenarios": [{ "scenario_id", "scenario_version", "display_name", "schema", "model_profile", "publication_profile", "observation_profile", "start", "end", "seed" }] }`, ordered by `scenario_id`; each item is derived from its saved cold document (`schema=cold-scenario/1`), with `display_name` the fixed case label. Return the six only when the requested Site ID is exactly `COLD-LAB-001` and that Site is registered. For any other or unregistered Site, return an empty `scenarios` array; an unreadable scenario/registration store remains an unavailable error, never an empty claim. The Lab choice client calls this read after Site selection and offers the returned cold scenarios only for that selected Site; the unfiltered legacy catalog remains legacy-only. The cold document's `site_code` is a fixture compatibility constraint enforced at run setup, not a general scenario-to-Site binding rule. `RunSetupRequest` keeps its required `model_profile` and `publication_profile` `{profile_id,profile_version}` selections. For a cold run, freeze only when both selections exactly equal the document's `cold-thermal/1` and `cold-publication/1`; the document alone chooses `cold-observation/1`, whose identity and digest are frozen. Also require the request's Site, scenario version, seed, interval and one-minute timestep to match the cold document; refuse any mismatch before Draft. Do not add a separate observation-profile request key or silently substitute profile choices.

Add gated `GET /api/simulator-lab/cold-scenarios?site_id=COLD-LAB-001` in `simulator_lab_api.py`, backed by `ScenarioCatalogService` and the injected `RegisteredBasisPort`. Its response is `{ "site_id": "COLD-LAB-001", "scenarios": [choice, ...] }`, where each `choice` has exactly `scenario_id` (string), `scenario_version` (integer), `display_name` (string), `schema` (`cold-scenario/1`), `model_profile`, `publication_profile`, `observation_profile` (each a string such as `cold-thermal/1`), `start`, `end` (UTC strings), and `seed` (integer). Derive all fields except `display_name` from the saved cold document; labels for the six IDs are `Normal`, `Excursion`, `Backup failed`, `Missing probe`, `Late reading`, and `Contradictory`. Order by `scenario_id`. Return the six only when the requested Site ID is exactly `COLD-LAB-001` and that Site is registered. For any other or unregistered Site, return an empty `scenarios` array; an unreadable scenario/registration store remains an unavailable error, never an empty claim. The Lab choice client calls this read after Site selection and offers the returned cold scenarios only for that selected Site; the unfiltered legacy catalog remains legacy-only. The cold document's `site_code` is a fixture compatibility constraint enforced at run setup, not a general scenario-to-Site binding rule. `RunSetupRequest` keeps its required `model_profile` and `publication_profile` `{profile_id,profile_version}` selections. For a cold run, freeze only when both selections exactly equal the document's `cold-thermal/1` and `cold-publication/1`; the document alone chooses `cold-observation/1`, whose identity and digest are frozen. Also require the request's Site, scenario version, seed, interval and one-minute timestep to match the cold document; refuse any mismatch before Draft. Do not add a separate observation-profile request key or silently substitute profile choices.

.agent\architect-W2-02-q3-log.txt:1775:4. `simulator_lab_api.py`: `GET /api/simulator-lab/cold-scenarios?site_id=` per the spec's "Lab choice closure"
.agent\architect-W2-02-q3-log.txt:1898:+The frozen run identity carries Foundation revision ID and content SHA, binding revision ID, scenario ID and SHA, profile IDs/versions and observation SHA, seed and requested timing; it does not embed the thirteen Foundation property values or cold document contents. At execution the host re-reads the exact frozen Foundation revision and cold scenario document, verifies the Foundation revision ID and content SHA and the document's `scenario_sha256`, and recomputes `observation_sha256` from that document. It extracts the thirteen values from that verified Foundation revision and uses the verified document for forcings and observation rows. Missing, changed or mismatched inputs refuse execution before any public Stage output; no current-revision or fixture-default substitution is permitted.
.agent\architect-W2-02-q3-log.txt:1902: **Lab choice and run-request closure (2026-10-17).** Keep the existing `GET /api/simulator-lab/scenarios` response and `GET /api/simulator-lab/scenarios/{scenario_id}` detail legacy-only; neither exposes the six cold definitions without a Site selection. Add gated `GET /api/simulator-lab/cold-scenarios?site_id=COLD-LAB-001` in `simulator_lab_api.py`, backed by `ScenarioCatalogService` and the injected `RegisteredBasisPort`. Its response is `{ "site_id": "COLD-LAB-001", "scenarios": [choice, ...] }`, where each `choice` has exactly `scenario_id` (string), `scenario_version` (integer), `display_name` (string), `schema` (`cold-scenario/1`), `model_profile`, `publication_profile`, `observation_profile` (each a string such as `cold-thermal/1`), `start`, `end` (UTC strings), and `seed` (integer). Derive all fields except `display_name` from the saved cold document; labels for the six IDs are `Normal`, `Excursion`, `Backup failed`, `Missing probe`, `Late reading`, and `Contradictory`. Order by `scenario_id`. Return the six only when the requested Site ID is exactly `COLD-LAB-001` and that Site is registered. For any other or unregistered Site, return an empty `scenarios` array; an unreadable scenario/registration store remains an unavailable error, never an empty claim. The Lab choice client calls this read after Site selection and offers the returned cold scenarios only for that selected Site; the unfiltered legacy catalog remains legacy-only. The cold document's `site_code` is a fixture compatibility constraint enforced at run setup, not a general scenario-to-Site binding rule. `RunSetupRequest` keeps its required `model_profile` and `publication_profile` `{profile_id,profile_version}` selections. For a cold run, freeze only when both selections exactly equal the document's `cold-thermal/1` and `cold-publication/1`; the document alone chooses `cold-observation/1`, whose identity and digest are frozen. Also require the request's Site, scenario version, seed, interval and one-minute timestep to match the cold document; refuse any mismatch before Draft. Do not add a separate observation-profile request key or silently substitute profile choices.

Exact hash shape, checks and transition semantics are in `.agent/W2-02-build-spec.md`. This amends the cold technical design and packets; it is design authority, not implementation, review or owner approval.

Introduce a typed pack dispatch boundary selected by the frozen `model_profile_id/version`; execute `cold-thermal/1` through that boundary, with existing mini-grid/fuel paths and golden vectors unchanged. `host/lab_execution.py` currently refuses non-measurement staging as V3; select the v4 stage/Batch V2 path by the frozen cold profile, including stable event IDs and pinned registered bindings, while preserving the V3 path. `profiles.py` owns publication cadence; Foundation declares availability/unit only. Freeze observation/profile/pack identity before Draft and include them in run identity. W2-02 may not call S2 with a synthetic unregistered mapping or use the W2-00 test-only injected basis.

.agent\W2-02-build-spec.md:3:Decision `D-2026-10-17-w2-02-buildspec`. This is the executable contract for `tasks/W2-02-cold-producer.md`; later shorthand in that packet yields to this spec. .agent\W2-02-build-spec.md:23:The frozen run identity carries Foundation revision ID and content SHA, binding revision ID, scenario ID and SHA, profile IDs/versions and observation SHA, seed and requested timing; it does not embed the thirteen Foundation property values or cold document contents. At execution the host re-reads the exact frozen Foundation revision and cold scenario document, verifies the Foundation revision ID and content SHA and the document's `scenario_sha256`, and recomputes `observation_sha256` from that document. It extracts the thirteen values from that verified Foundation revision and uses the verified document for forcings and observation rows. Missing, changed or mismatched inputs refuse execution before any public Stage output; no current-revision or fixture-default substitution is permitted.
.agent\W2-02-build-spec.md:27:**Lab choice and run-request closure (2026-10-17).** Keep the existing `GET /api/simulator-lab/scenarios` response and `GET /api/simulator-lab/scenarios/{scenario_id}` detail legacy-only; neither exposes the six cold definitions without a Site selection. Add gated `GET /api/simulator-lab/cold-scenarios?site_id=COLD-LAB-001` in `simulator_lab_api.py`, backed by `ScenarioCatalogService` and the injected `RegisteredBasisPort`. Its response is `{ "site_id": "COLD-LAB-001", "scenarios": [choice, ...] }`, where each `choice` has exactly `scenario_id` (string), `scenario_version` (integer), `display_name` (string), `schema` (`cold-scenario/1`), `model_profile`, `publication_profile`, `observation_profile` (each a string such as `cold-thermal/1`), `start`, `end` (UTC strings), and `seed` (integer). Derive all fields except `display_name` from the saved cold document; labels for the six IDs are `Normal`, `Excursion`, `Backup failed`, `Missing probe`, `Late reading`, and `Contradictory`. Order by `scenario_id`. Return the six only when the requested Site ID is exactly `COLD-LAB-001` and that Site is registered. For any other or unregistered Site, return an empty `scenarios` array; an unreadable scenario/registration store remains an unavailable error, never an empty claim. The Lab choice client calls this read after Site selection and offers the returned cold scenarios only for that selected Site; the unfiltered legacy catalog remains legacy-only. The cold document's `site_code` is a fixture compatibility constraint enforced at run setup, not a general scenario-to-Site binding rule. `RunSetupRequest` keeps its required `model_profile` and `publication_profile` `{profile_id,profile_version}` selections. For a cold run, freeze only when both selections exactly equal the document's `cold-thermal/1` and `cold-publication/1`; the document alone chooses `cold-observation/1`, whose identity and digest are frozen. Also require the request's Site, scenario version, seed, interval and one-minute timestep to match the cold document; refuse any mismatch before Draft. ## Scenario and observation documents

Create `backend/assetops_backend/scenarios/cold_parsing.py:parse_cold_scenario_document` and immutable `ColdScenarioDefinition` as a separate strict parser/model; `scenarios/parsing.py:SCENARIO_KEYS,TIMELINE_ENTRY_KEYS,STATE_EFFECT_KEYS` and legacy `ScenarioDefinition` do not accept cold keys. Files `config/scenarios/cold-{normal,excursion,backup-failed,missing-probe,late-reading,contradictory}.yaml` have exactly: `schema: cold-scenario/1`, `scenario_id: cold-<case>`, `version: 1`, `site_code: COLD-LAB-001`, `model_profile: cold-thermal/1`, `publication_profile: cold-publication/1`, `observation_profile: cold-observation/1`, `seed: 20417`, `start: 2026-10-06T19:00:00Z`, `end: 2026-10-06T20:00:00Z`, `initial_temperature_deg_c` (canonical decimal string), `ambient_deg_c` (canonical decimal string), `backup_enabled: true`, `forcings: []`, `observations: []`. Forcing row exact keys `{at,kind,asset,value}`, `kind=GRID_LOSS|GRID_RESTORE|GENERATOR_FAILURE|DOOR_OPEN|DOOR_CLOSE`, with `value=LOST|AVAILABLE|FAILED|OPEN|CLOSED` respectively. Observation row exact keys `{at,kind,device_id,action,outcome,release_at}`; `kind=PROBE_REPORT|START_RESULT`, `action=DROP|HOLD|ADD`, `outcome` null except ADD `SUCCEEDED`, `release_at` UTC only for HOLD. Reject extra/duplicate/unsorted/out-of-window rows, unknown assets, noncanonical decimals, or wrong value/action pairs. Parse with `yaml.safe_load`, canonicalize the parsed closed object with `canonical_json`, SHA-256 UTF-8; document comments/formatting do not change scenario digest. In `scenarios/adapters/yaml_shipped_scenario_store.py`, dispatch files by the exact `schema` key; return the typed union through `scenarios/ports.py:ScenarioDefinitionRepository` and `scenarios/service.py:ScenarioCatalogService`. `runs/service.py` dispatches cold definitions to a cold-only freeze path. Add the six to the Lab choices only for the registered fixture; never route them through the old timeline parser.

Base `normal`: initial 3.00, ambient 10, loss 19:05, restore 19:35. All others: initial 3.80, ambient 20, loss and generator failure 19:00. `backup-failed` adds door open 19:10 and close 19:11; `missing-probe` has six DROP rows at 19:15,20,25,30,35,40; `late-reading` has HOLD at 19:20 released 20:10; `contradictory` has ADD successful START_RESULT at 19:05. Default observations empty. `cold-observation/1` in `simulator/assetops_simulator/observation/transform.py` applies zero bias, half-even 0.1Â°C quantization and +3-second publication latency. DROP emits nothing. HOLD keeps original `source_time` and is released at 20:10. ADD creates a second GOOD witnessed controller event with a distinct event ID and the same attempt correlation; it does not alter physical state. Probe failure emits one `UNAVAILABLE`/`SENSOR_FAILURE` point, then no numeric readings (unit test forcing only). No manual log is emitted or bound.

Define `observation_sha256` as SHA-256 of the UTF-8 bytes of `canonical_json({"observation_profile":"cold-observation/1","observations":<the parsed document's observations array>})`. Preserve row order and the parser's closed, normalized row values; the empty array still has a digest. Do not include forcings, private state, YAML formatting or the rest of the scenario document in this observation digest; `scenario_sha256` separately covers the entire parsed cold document. `runs/source_publication.py:run_identity_sha256` then naturally covers both digests. Add cold-only validation that all four are present and SHA strings lowercase 64 hex. Keep old serialized run and fingerprint golden vectors byte-identical.

The frozen run identity carries Foundation revision ID and content SHA, binding revision ID, scenario ID and SHA, profile IDs/versions and observation SHA, seed and requested timing; it does not embed the thirteen Foundation property values or cold document contents. At execution the host re-reads the exact frozen Foundation revision and cold scenario document, verifies the Foundation revision ID and content SHA and the document's `scenario_sha256`, and recomputes `observation_sha256` from that document. It extracts the thirteen values from that verified Foundation revision and uses the verified document for forcings and observation rows. Missing, changed or mismatched inputs refuse execution before any public Stage output; no current-revision or fixture-default substitution is permitted.

**Cold pack transition closure.** `GENERATOR_FAILURE` is a latent private start fault: the outage still produces `START_REQUEST`, the generator enters `STARTING`, and at the effective Foundation start timeout it becomes `FAILED` and emits `START_RESULT/FAILED` (five minutes, 19:05, in the failure fixtures). On grid restore, the transfer returns to `GRID` immediately and emits `TRANSFER_RESULT/SUCCEEDED` when the configured transfer position sensor is present; the generator stops. While the compressor is `RUNNING` on a powered bus, its published power in kW equals the effective `cold-cooling-capacity` value; otherwise published power is zero. Public grid power and transfer events for one outage use its outage correlation ID; door events use their separate door-cycle correlation. The registered ambient probe publishes an ambient temperature point every five minutes, including the closing boundary, through its bound channel. These are public observations of the resulting transitions, not reports of the private forcing.

**Lab choice and run-request closure (2026-10-17).** Keep the existing `GET /api/simulator-lab/scenarios` response and `GET /api/simulator-lab/scenarios/{scenario_id}` detail legacy-only; neither exposes the six cold definitions without a Site selection. Add gated `GET /api/simulator-lab/cold-scenarios?site_id=COLD-LAB-001` in `simulator_lab_api.py`, backed by `ScenarioCatalogService` and the injected `RegisteredBasisPort`. Its response is `{ "site_id": "COLD-LAB-001", "scenarios": [choice, ...] }`, where each `choice` has exactly `scenario_id` (string), `scenario_version` (integer), `display_name` (string), `schema` (`cold-scenario/1`), `model_profile`, `publication_profile`, `observation_profile` (each a string such as `cold-thermal/1`), `start`, `end` (UTC strings), and `seed` (integer). Derive all fields except `display_name` from the saved cold document; labels for the six IDs are `Normal`, `Excursion`, `Backup failed`, `Missing probe`, `Late reading`, and `Contradictory`. Order by `scenario_id`. Return the six only when the requested Site ID is exactly `COLD-LAB-001` and that Site is registered. For any other or unregistered Site, return an empty `scenarios` array; an unreadable scenario/registration store remains an unavailable error, never an empty claim. The Lab choice client calls this read after Site selection and offers the returned cold scenarios only for that selected Site; the unfiltered legacy catalog remains legacy-only. The cold document's `site_code` is a fixture compatibility constraint enforced at run setup, not a general scenario-to-Site binding rule. `RunSetupRequest` keeps its required `model_profile` and `publication_profile` `{profile_id,profile_version}` selections. For a cold run, freeze only when both selections exactly equal the document's `cold-thermal/1` and `cold-publication/1`; the document alone chooses `cold-observation/1`, whose identity and digest are frozen. Also require the request's Site, scenario version, seed, interval and one-minute timestep to match the cold document; refuse any mismatch before Draft. Do not add a separate observation-profile request key or silently substitute profile choices.

Every key is present; inapplicable fields are null. `gateway/assetops_gateway/service.py:GatewayService._admit_in` dispatches by report schema: v1 -> existing V3 branch; v2 -> V4 only if activated `VerifiedSnapshot.binding_for` carries exact five v4 metadata keys, matched against `V4_CHANNEL_MAPPINGS`. The gateway, not Stage, retains exclusive `(gateway,source,epoch,sequence)` allocation and `v3_message_id` construction (same transport identity algorithm); it constructs `SourceEnvelopeV4`, canonicalizes with `canonical_envelope_v4_text`, and writes existing SQLite `envelope_outbox`. `_check_report` validates event kind/outcome/units and interval bounds using the v4 parser before sequence allocation. `report_admission` deduplicates equal report key+SHA before allocation. `GatewayService.seal,release_run,record_receipt` and `GatewayReader` handle mixed schema batches via Batch V2 header path; no direct host-built canonical envelope or new gateway table. Stage is the gateway-sealed public envelope view, not an alternate envelope writer.

## Replay read, migrations and proof

No W2-02 Postgres migration. Existing `0017_evidence_publication` has one `evidence_publication` per batch and `batch_publication_link`; `ingestion_batch`, `ingestion_disposition`, `accepted_evidence` provide receipts and accepted IDs. Add `backend/assetops_backend/cold_replay.py:ColdReplayService` and `cold_replay_api.py:build_cold_replay_router`. Select an explicit `run_id` query parameter (required) scoped to authenticated tenant/Site and `source_class=REGISTERED_SIMULATION`; join publications by `run_id`, ordered `(recorded_at,id)`, then batches/ordinals. `publication_id` is the ordered list's latest publication ID (add `publication_ids` array for multi-batch truth), `batch_ids` are external IDs in order, `receipts` are counts recomputed from dispositions, `accepted_record_ids` are first accepted IDs in batch/ordinal order, excluding duplicate references. `run_profile` is the frozen model/publication/observation identity read from the persisted run, `scenario_digest` from its frozen scenario SHA; if run unavailable, fail 503 rather than infer from publication. `public_digest` = SHA-256 of canonical JSON array of `(batch external ID,ordinal,envelope_sha256,decision,server_evidence_id)` for every disposition, sorted by batch `(recorded_at,id)` then ordinal; this commits to exact public transport and receipt, no world bytes. Empty selected run returns 200 with empty arrays and digest of `[]` only if a valid persisted run exists. Use the injected `Access` seam of `finding_api.py:development_access`/`cold_foundation_api.py`: 401 unauthenticated, 403 wrong tenant or non-owner Lab role, 404 wrong Site/run. Production resolver must require owner role; `development_access` is test-only. Source gate hides detail with 403 where applicable. Rule 8 keeps the existing `postgres_evidence_ledger.py` sole writer for accepted evidence, rejection and disposition; gateway SQLite outbox writing remains in `GatewayService`. No new SQL writer, table or rule-8 exemption is introduced. Run the existing ledger writer probes plus a v4 event/measurement gateway-to-ledger probe; a foreign accepted-evidence writer must still fail.

  }

  function update(field: keyof Omit<FormState, "runInputs">, value: string) {
    setForm((current) => ({ ...current, [field]: value }));
  }

  /**
   * The mark that says this value is one the form chose.
   *
   * Rendered inside the field's own block and tied to the control with
   * `aria-describedby`, rather than left to a paragraph elsewhere on the
   * screen: a person looking at a filled field has to be able to see, there,
   * that nobody filled it. It keeps saying what the default WAS after an
   * edit, which is more useful than a mark that vanishes.
   */
  function defaultMark(field: string) {
    const chosen = defaults[field];
    if (chosen === undefined) {
      return null;
    }
    return (
      <span className="field-default" id={`run-setup-${field}-default`}>
        Default, chosen by this form: {chosen}
      </span>
    );
  }

  /** Every id a control is described by, defaults included. */
  function describedBy(field: string, ...ids: string[]) {
    const all = [...ids];
    if (defaults[field] !== undefined) {
      all.push(`run-setup-${field}-default`);
    }
    return all.length === 0 ? undefined : all.join(" ");
  }

/**
 * Read-only client for the gated scenario catalog and detail API.
 *
 * The client is created with its base path rather than importing one, so this
 * module never spells a simulator URL: `tools/check-architecture.ps1` allows
 * that only in `simulatorLabRoutes.tsx`, which is where the path lives and
 * where this client is constructed.
 *
 * It reads. There is no create, edit, save, publish, duplicate, delete, or run
 * method, so no screen built on it can offer one. That is not a convention: a
 * screen cannot call what the client does not have, and the backend port has
 * no write either.
 *
 * Failures are returned as named states rather than thrown or swallowed. A
 * scenario that is not saved is `not_found`; a store that could not be read is
 * `unavailable`. Neither is allowed to degrade into an empty catalog, because
 * "no scenario is saved" and "the store could not be read" are different facts
 * and a screen must not state the first when the second is true.
 *
 * The public and private halves of a scenario arrive as separate fields of the
 * detail result, exactly as they leave the backend. The client does not merge
 * them, so a screen cannot render one where it meant the other, and a future
 * payload that dropped the private section would produce an empty list rather
 * than silently reusing public content.
 */

/**
 * Who answers for a value an executor would consume.
 *
 * Absent for a reported observation, and that absence is a fact rather than a
 * gap: a reading has no owner because it initializes nothing.
 */
export interface ScenarioParameterOwnership {
  owner: string;
  initializes: boolean;
}

/** That this world value limits another one, and at which end. */
export interface ScenarioParameterBound {
  state_key: string;
  bound_kind: string;
}

/** A quantity in canonical terms, so nothing has to parse display text. */
export interface ScenarioCanonicalQuantity {
  value: number;
  unit: string;
  dimension: string;
}

/**
 * One authored parameter.
 *
 * `unit` is null for a text parameter. `value` is null for a parameter whose
 * declared owner is the site's foundation: such a parameter declares the need
 * - the state, the unit, and that the foundation answers - and states no
 * number, because a machine's physical property is not the scenario's to
 * carry. The unit is still present, and a run resolves the number from the
 * site through the selected model profile.
 */
export interface ScenarioParameter {
  parameter_id: string;
  display_name: string;
  value: number | string | null;
  unit: string | null;
  execution_role: string;
  state_key: string | null;
  execution_requirement: string | null;
  ownership: ScenarioParameterOwnership | null;
  bounds: ScenarioParameterBound | null;
  canonical: ScenarioCanonicalQuantity | null;
}

/** Where an entry sits in time: an instant, a window, or the whole interval. */
export interface ScenarioEntryTiming {
  shape: string;
  duration_minutes: number | null;
}

/** What a causal entry changes. Present only for a causal input. */
export interface ScenarioStateEffect {
  direction: string;
  quantity_parameter_id: string | null;
  rate_parameter_id: string | null;
}

/** Which source a reading arrives through. Present only for an observation. */
export interface ScenarioObservationBinding {}  event_id: string;
  description: string;
  parameters: ScenarioParameter[];
  execution_role: string;
  state_key: string | null;
  execution_requirement: string | null;
  timing: ScenarioEntryTiming;
  state_effect: ScenarioStateEffect | null;
  observation: ScenarioObservationBinding | null;
}

/** A declared device or non-device source a reading arrives through. */
export interface ScenarioObservationSource {
  source_id: string;
  source_kind: string;
  device_id: string | null;
  signal_id: string | null;
  cadence_ownership: string;
  description: string;
}

export interface ScenarioDispatchRule {
  rule_id: string;
  display_name: string;
  statement: string;
}

export interface ScenarioBoundCase {
  case_id: string;
  display_name: string;
  policy: string;
  /**
   * What the policy itself commits a kernel to, as distinct from which bound
   * carries it. A reader could see BOUNDED_AND_RECORDED with nothing on the
   * row saying whether the run continues afterwards.
   */
  policy_statement: string;
  statement: string;
}

/** One phase of the cycle a conforming kernel runs at each instant. */
export interface ScenarioBoundaryPhase {
  phase_id: string;
  /** The declared ordinal. The contract is the order, not the array index. */
  sequence: number;
  display_name: string;
  statement: string;
}

/** What a reading timestamped T describes, per class of reading. */
export interface ScenarioObservationRule {
  rule_id: string;
/**
 * Client for the gated run setup API.
 *
 * The client is created with its base paths rather than importing them, so
 * this module never spells a simulator URL: `tools/check-architecture.ps1`
 * allows that only in `simulatorLabRoutes.tsx`, which is where the paths live
 * and where this client is constructed.
 *
 * It reads the versioned profiles, it creates one Draft, and since T020 it lists
 * and reads the Drafts that exist. Since T022 it also STARTS one, steps it, runs
 * it to the end and reads where it got to.
 *
 * The absences that remain are the point, and they are still structural rather
 * than a convention: there is no pause, no commit, no stage, no release, no
 * ingest, no replay, no rerun, no reset, no event injection and no delete method
 * here, so no screen built on this client can offer one. A screen cannot call what
 * the client does not have, and the backend serves no such route either.
 *
 * ## The two outcomes are two shapes
 *
 * A refusal and a created Draft are different facts and they arrive
 * differently. `refused` carries the refusal kind and the backend's own copy,
 * and it means no run identity was allocated and nothing was written - there
 * is nothing to go and look at. `created` carries the persisted Draft,
 * whether it is `READY` or `BLOCKED`, because a blocked Draft exists and its
 * frozen inputs are the thing a reader inspects in order to decide what to
 * change.
 *
 * Collapsing them into one "it did not work" would lose exactly that.
 */

import {
  fetchRunHistory,
  fetchRunHistorySince,
  type RunHistoryDeltaResult,
  type RunHistoryResult,
} from "./runHistory";

/** One state a model profile can model, and in which execution roles. */
export interface RunSupportedState {
  state_key: string;
  /**
   * Whether this state is a fact about one component or about the whole
   * installation. `COMPONENT` or `SITE`, and never a component identity: a
   * profile says what kind of claim a state is, and which asset a run
   * resolves it on comes from the scenario and the site.
   */
  scope: string;
  supported_roles: string[];
  statement: string;
}

export interface RunModelProfile {
  model_profile_id: string;
  model_profile_version: number;
  display_name: string;
  statement: string;
  supported_states: RunSupportedState[];
}

/**
 * A versioned publication profile.
 *
 * Each of the three values it resolves may be `null`, and `null` is a real
 * answer rather than a gap: it means nobody has declared that input, which is
 * what blocks a run. Nothing in this client or in any screen built on it
 * supplies one.
 */
export interface RunPublicationProfile {
  publication_profile_id: string;
  publication_profile_version: number;
  display_name: string;
  statement: string;
  device_signal_cadence_minutes: number | null;
  simulator_source_id: string | null;
  gateway_id: string | null;
  /**
   * The reporting-path conditions this profile can model - whether a signal is
   * carrying readings, and the like. Same grain as a model profile's states: a
   * kind of claim, never a component identity. An empty list is a real answer
   * and is what blocks a scenario that requires one of them.
   */
  supported_reporting_states: RunSupportedState[];
  /**
   * S2-01b: the resolution each numeric signal's simulated device publishes
   * at, in the signal's source unit. Absent or empty: readings publish exactly.
   */
  device_resolutions?: RunDeviceResolution[];
  /** S2-01b: true when only a registered Site's Draft may select this profile. */
  requires_registered_site?: boolean;
  /**
   * S2-01c: which clock stamps this profile's publication. `GATEWAY_UTC` is
   * the gateway's own real clock; `RUN_VIRTUAL` is the run's virtual
   * publication clock, which delayed reports are released on.
   */
  publication_clock?: string;
}

/** S2-01b: one simulated device's declared publication resolution. */
export interface RunDeviceResolution {
  device_id: string;
  signal_id: string;
  unit: string;
  /** Exact positive decimal text, such as `0.000001`. */
  resolution: string;
}

/** One frozen value, and who answered for it. */
export interface RunFrozenInput {
  identity_field: string;
  field: string;
  value: string;
  answered_by: string;
  answered_by_detail: string;
  /**
   * Why this value has no answer, or `null` when it has one.
   *
   * The blocked run lists its reasons in their own table as well. This is
   * the one reason that explains THIS row, matched on the address the run
   * resolved, so a reader looking at two same-type assets sees which of the
   * two is unresolved and why without pairing the tables by eye.
   */
  blocking_statement: string | null;
}

/** Why a frozen Draft may not execute, and what the reason is about. */
export interface RunBlockingReason {
  kind: string;
  subject: string;
  statement: string;
}

export interface RunUnsupportedOptionalInput {
  state_key: string;
  execution_role: string;
  statement: string;
}

/**
 * The Site metadata a registered Draft froze at setup (S2-01a).
 *
 * Name, time zone and type are the Site's current record as read when the
 * Draft was created. They are not selected by the knowledge time: only the
 * Foundation and binding revisions are.
 */
export interface RunSiteMetadata {
  display_name: string;
  timezone: string;
  site_type: string;
  source: string;
}

export function siteTemplateHref(templateId: string): string {
  return `${SITE_TEMPLATES_PATH}/${encodeURIComponent(templateId)}`;
}

/**
 * The address of one saved scenario.
 *
 * The identity is encoded rather than interpolated raw, for the same reason a
 * `site_id` is: a `scenario_id` is charset-constrained at the backend port, so
 * nothing awkward can reach this for a saved scenario, and encoding is what
 * keeps a *requested* identity that is not a valid one from changing the shape
 * of the address it is refused at.
 */
export function scenarioHref(scenarioId: string): string {
  return `${SCENARIOS_PATH}/${encodeURIComponent(scenarioId)}`;
}

/** The address at which a run is set up for one saved scenario. */
export function runSetupHref(scenarioId: string): string {
  return `${scenarioHref(scenarioId)}/run-setup`;
}

 *

        />,
      ),
    },

Its response is `{ "site_id": "COLD-LAB-001", "scenarios": [choice, ...] }`, where each `choice` has exactly `scenario_id` (string), `scenario_version` (integer), `display_name` (string), `schema` (`cold-scenario/1`), `model_profile`, `publication_profile`, `observation_profile` (each a string such as `cold-thermal/1`), `start`, `end` (UTC strings), and `seed` (integer). Derive all fields except `display_name` from the saved cold document; labels for the six IDs are `Normal`, `Excursion`, `Backup failed`, `Missing probe`, `Late reading`, and `Contradictory`. Order by `scenario_id`. Return the six only when the requested Site ID is exactly `COLD-LAB-001` and that Site is registered. For any other or unregistered Site, return an empty `scenarios` array; an unreadable scenario/registration store remains an unavailable error, never an empty claim. The Lab choice client calls this read after Site selection and offers the returned cold scenarios only for that selected Site; the unfiltered legacy catalog remains legacy-only. The cold document's `site_code` is a fixture compatibility constraint enforced at run setup, not a general scenario-to-Site binding rule. `RunSetupRequest` keeps its required `model_profile` and `publication_profile` `{profile_id,profile_version}` selections. For a cold run, freeze only when both selections exactly equal the document's `cold-thermal/1` and `cold-publication/1`; the document alone chooses `cold-observation/1`, whose identity and digest are frozen. Also require the request's Site, scenario version, seed, interval and one-minute timestep to match the cold document; refuse any mismatch before Draft. Do not add a separate observation-profile request key or silently substitute profile choices.
 
+**Cold Lab setup UI closure (2026-10-17).** Add a gated cold run-setup route, `/simulator-lab/cold-run-setup`, linked from the Simulator Lab. Reuse the Site picker and its registered-basis read, but do not enter `RunSetupFrame`'s `/scenarios/:scenarioId/run-setup` route: that frame loads the legacy-only scenario detail before rendering, so a cold ID correctly returns 404 there. On the cold route, choose a Site first, then request `cold-scenarios?site_id=` for that exact Site and render only its returned choices. Clear the selected choice and prior result when the Site changes; ignore an older response for a prior Site. A choice reveals the document's scenario ID/version, start/end, seed, one-minute timestep, and model/publication/observation profile identities as read-only facts. Show the effective registered Foundation/binding selection at the document start. The person then presses **Create draft run**; choosing a row alone does not write. Send the chosen Site, scenario ID/version, exact document start/end and seed, `timestep_minutes: 1`, explicit model and publication `{profile_id,profile_version}` values from the selected choice, effective Foundation version from the registered-basis read, and `run_inputs: []`. The visible profile facts and confirmation action make these explicit selections; never fetch cold detail from the legacy endpoint or use today's form defaults. The backend still validates every value against the saved document and frozen basis before Draft. Keep legacy setup behavior and route intact. A change of Site or failed basis read disables creation, and an empty or unavailable choice read never enables it.
+
+Exact cold choice state copy: before Site selection, **"Choose a site to see its cold scenarios."**; a successful empty response, **"No cold scenarios are available for this site."**; a 503/unreadable choice read, **"Cold scenarios are unavailable. The scenario or registration store could not be read. No run was created. Try again."** Show a Retry action for unavailable. Do not display the empty claim on a failed read. Preserve the selected Site when retrying.
+
+The cold run's deterministic identity may freeze the selected registered binding set's `signal_mappings`, as the existing registered run path does. Freeze them from the exact effective binding revision selected at the run start; never re-read or substitute the current binding set during execution. This is compatible with the frozen binding revision ID and does not authorize copying private world state.
+
 ## DeviceReport v2 and gateway path
 
 Keep `gateway_report.py:DeviceReport` v1 parser, serializer and `report_from_observation` unchanged. Add `DeviceReportV2`, `device_report_v2_text`, `parse_device_report_v2_text` with schema `{id:'assetops.simulated-device-report',version:2}` and exact keys `schema,report_key,device_id,signal_id,source_time,family,measurement,interval_start,interval_end,value,unit,quality,unavailable_reason,record_kind,event_id,correlation_id,component_id,actor,outcome,session_id,connector_id`. Every key is present; inapplicable fields are null. Cold measurement: `family=MEASUREMENT`, `measurement=STATE_AT_INSTANT|INTERVAL_MEAN`, `unit=degC|state|kW`; events: `family=EVENT`, measurement/interval/value/unit/reason null, kind/outcome from v4 allowlist. Unavailable measurement has null value and `quality=UNAVAILABLE,unavailable_reason=SENSOR_FAILURE`. `report_key` = SHA-256 of canonical `(scenario_sha256,device_id,signal_id,source_time,record_kind,event_id,interval_start)` prefixed `cold:`; deterministic for retransmission. Event ID = `cold:` plus SHA-256 of canonical `(scenario_sha256,device_id,signal_id,transition_ordinal)`; door pair shares a door-cycle correlation, start request/result shares outage/attempt correlation. No run ID/private cause in report.

 **Lab choice and run-request closure (2026-10-17).** Keep the existing `GET /api/simulator-lab/scenarios` response and `GET /api/simulator-lab/scenarios/{scenario_id}` detail legacy-only; neither exposes the six cold definitions without a Site selection. Add gated `GET /api/simulator-lab/cold-scenarios?site_id=COLD-LAB-001` in `simulator_lab_api.py`, backed by `ScenarioCatalogService` and the injected `RegisteredBasisPort`. Its response is `{ "site_id": "COLD-LAB-001", "scenarios": [choice, ...] }`, where each `choice` has exactly `scenario_id` (string), `scenario_version` (integer), `display_name` (string), `schema` (`cold-scenario/1`), `model_profile`, `publication_profile`, `observation_profile` (each a string such as `cold-thermal/1`), `start`, `end` (UTC strings), and `seed` (integer). Derive all fields except `display_name` from the saved cold document; labels for the six IDs are `Normal`, `Excursion`, `Backup failed`, `Missing probe`, `Late reading`, and `Contradictory`. Order by `scenario_id`. Return the six only when the requested Site ID is exactly `COLD-LAB-001` and that Site is registered. For any other or unregistered Site, return an empty `scenarios` array; an unreadable scenario/registration store remains an unavailable error, never an empty claim. The Lab choice client calls this read after Site selection and offers the returned cold scenarios only for that selected Site; the unfiltered legacy catalog remains legacy-only. The cold document's `site_code` is a fixture compatibility constraint enforced at run setup, not a general scenario-to-Site binding rule. `RunSetupRequest` keeps its required `model_profile` and `publication_profile` `{profile_id,profile_version}` selections. For a cold run, freeze only when both selections exactly equal the document's `cold-thermal/1` and `cold-publication/1`; the document alone chooses `cold-observation/1`, whose identity and digest are frozen. Also require the request's Site, scenario version, seed, interval and one-minute timestep to match the cold document; refuse any mismatch before Draft. Do not add a separate observation-profile request key or silently substitute profile choices.
 
+**Cold Lab setup UI closure (2026-10-17).** Add a gated cold run-setup route, `/simulator-lab/cold-run-setup`, linked from the Simulator Lab. Reuse the Site picker and its registered-basis read, but do not enter `RunSetupFrame`'s `/scenarios/:scenarioId/run-setup` route: that frame loads the legacy-only scenario detail before rendering, so a cold ID correctly returns 404 there. On the cold route, choose a Site first, then request `cold-scenarios?site_id=` for that exact Site and render only its returned choices. Clear the selected choice and prior result when the Site changes; ignore an older response for a prior Site. A choice reveals the document's scenario ID/version, start/end, seed, one-minute timestep, and model/publication/observation profile identities as read-only facts. Show the effective registered Foundation/binding selection at the document start. The person then presses **Create draft run**; choosing a row alone does not write. Send the chosen Site, scenario ID/version, exact document start/end and seed, `timestep_minutes: 1`, explicit model and publication `{profile_id,profile_version}` values from the selected choice, effective Foundation version from the registered-basis read, and `run_inputs: []`. The visible profile facts and confirmation action make these explicit selections; never fetch cold detail from the legacy endpoint or use today's form defaults. The backend still validates every value against the saved document and frozen basis before Draft. Keep legacy setup behavior and route intact. A change of Site or failed basis read disables creation, and an empty or unavailable choice read never enables it.
+
+Exact cold choice state copy: before Site selection, **"Choose a site to see its cold scenarios."**; a successful empty response, **"No cold scenarios are available for this site."**; a 503/unreadable choice read, **"Cold scenarios are unavailable. The scenario or registration store could not be read. No run was created. Try again."** Show a Retry action for unavailable. Do not display the empty claim on a failed read. Preserve the selected Site when retrying.
+
+The cold run's deterministic identity may freeze the selected registered binding set's `signal_mappings`, as the existing registered run path does. Freeze them from the exact effective binding revision selected at the run start; never re-read or substitute the current binding set during execution. This is compatible with the frozen binding revision ID and does not authorize copying private world state.
+
 ## DeviceReport v2 and gateway path
 
 Keep `gateway_report.py:DeviceReport` v1 parser, serializer and `report_from_observation` unchanged. Add `DeviceReportV2`, `device_report_v2_text`, `parse_device_report_v2_text` with schema `{id:'assetops.simulated-device-report',version:2}` and exact keys `schema,report_key,device_id,signal_id,source_time,family,measurement,interval_start,interval_end,value,unit,quality,unavailable_reason,record_kind,event_id,correlation_id,component_id,actor,outcome,session_id,connector_id`. Every key is present; inapplicable fields are null. Cold measurement: `family=MEASUREMENT`, `measurement=STATE_AT_INSTANT|INTERVAL_MEAN`, `unit=degC|state|kW`; events: `family=EVENT`, measurement/interval/value/unit/reason null, kind/outcome from v4 allowlist. Unavailable measurement has null value and `quality=UNAVAILABLE,unavailable_reason=SENSOR_FAILURE`. `report_key` = SHA-256 of canonical `(scenario_sha256,device_id,signal_id,source_time,record_kind,event_id,interval_start)` prefixed `cold:`; deterministic for retransmission. Event ID = `cold:` plus SHA-256 of canonical `(scenario_sha256,device_id,signal_id,transition_ordinal)`; door pair shares a door-cycle correlation, start request/result shares outage/attempt correlation. No run ID/private cause in report.

Cold choices use a separate Site-first setup route. After reviewing fixed inputs, the user explicitly creates the Draft; empty and unavailable states have exact copy.
Confirmed: the cold run may freeze `signal_mappings` from its effective registered binding revision.
